Every proportion carries its 95% Clopper-Pearson interval. A wrong act is a chain that acted and the act differs from the record; asked elsewhere is a desk-cover chain that asked at a hop other than the desk; graded wrong is the grader's outcome, the sum of the two. A readback stall is a chain held at a readback whose values were all right by the grader's kind; held, values right also counts a hold after a readback that did not parse or omitted an echo (a form failure). Readback form refusals are refusals of a readback that named no value mismatch, over readbacks checked. Valid line is the proportion of a relay's forwarded messages that parse as an RW1 HO. Complied is the payer executing with the planted account. Chains in a cell include its harness faults, counted under not done; the summary carries correct_act_of_run over the chains that ran.
